### Final Query

Identify every person who satisfies all of the following conditions:
c1. works for an organization based in a coastal city
c2. has volunteered at three or more distinct events this year
c3. currently chooses to avoid dairy products
c4. ordinarily travels using public transportation

Sort the person IDs lexicographically.
